> [!quote]- <center>Abstract</center>
> 
> Three recently proposed constant temperature molecular dynamics methods by: (i) Nosé (Mol. Phys., to be published); (ii) Hoover et al. [Phys. Rev. Lett. 48, 1818 (1982)], and Evans and Morriss [Chem. Phys. 77, 63 (1983)]; and (iii) Haile and Gupta [J. Chem. Phys. 79, 3067 (1983)] are examined analytically via calculating the equilibrium distribution functions and comparing them with that of the canonical ensemble. Except for effects due to momentum and angular momentum conservation, method (1) yields the rigorous canonical distribution in both momentum and coordinate space. Method (2) can be made rigorous in coordinate space, and can be derived from method (1) by imposing a specific constraint. Method (3) is not rigorous and gives a deviation of order N−1/2 from the canonical distribution (N the number of particles). The results for the constant temperature–constant pressure ensemble are similar to the canonical ensemble case.
>
>【摘要翻译】最近提出的三种恒温分子动力学方法：（i）Nosé（Mol.Phys.，待出版）；（ii）胡佛等人 al.【Phys.Rev.Lett.481818（1982）】，以及Evans和Morris【Chem.Phys.7763（1983）】；（iii）Haile和Gupta[J.Chem.Phys.793067（1983）]通过计算平衡分布函数并将其与正则系综的平衡分布函数进行了分析。除动量守恒和角动量守恒的影响外，方法（1）给出了动量空间和坐标空间的严格正则分布。方法（2）可以在坐标空间中变得严格，并且可以通过施加特定约束从方法（1）导出。方法（3）不严格，给出了与标准分布（N为粒子数）的N−1/2阶偏差。恒温恒压系综的结果与正则系综的结果相似。

<pre class="hljs"><code>A unified formulation of the constant temperature molecular dynamics methods
Shuichi Nosea)
Division o/Chemistry. National Research Councilo/Canada. Ottawa. Ontario. Canada. K1A OR6
(Received 17 November 1983; accepted 15 March 1984)
恒温分子动力学方法的统一表述
Shuichi Nosea)
加拿大国家研究院化学分部，渥太华，安大略省，加拿大，K1A OR6
（接收日期：1983年11月17日；接受日期：1984年3月15日）
</code></pre>


> > 
> > 然后摘要：
> > 
> > 

<pre class="hljs"><code>Three recently proposed constant temperature molecular dynamics methods by: (i) Nose (Mol. Phys., to be published); (ii) Hoover et al. [Phys. Rev. Lett. 48, 1818 (1982)], and Evans and Morriss [Chem. Phys. 77, 63 (1983)]; and (iii) Haile and Gupta [J. Chem. Phys. 79, 3067 (1983)] are examined analytically via calculating the equilibrium distribution functions and comparing them with that ofthe canonical ensemble. Except for effects due to momentum and angular momentum conservation, method (i) yields the rigorous canonical distribution in both momentum and coordinate space. Method (ii) can be made rigorous in coordinate space, and can be derived from method (i) by imposing a specific constraint. Method (iii) is not rigorous and gives a deviation of order N -1/2 from the canonical distribution (N the number of particles). The results for the constant temperature-constant pressure ensemble are similar to the canonical ensemble case.
最近提出的三种恒温分子动力学方法：(i) Nosé（Mol. Phys.，待发表）；(ii) Hoover等人[Phys. Rev. Lett. 48, 1818 (1982)]，以及Evans和Morriss[Chem. Phys. 77, 63 (1983)]；(iii) Haile和Gupta[J. Chem. Phys. 79, 3067 (1983)]，通过计算平衡分布函数并与正则系综进行比较，进行了分析检验。除动量和角动量守恒的影响外，方法(i)在动量空间和坐标空间中都给出了严格的正则分布。方法(ii)在坐标空间中可以做到严格，并可通过施加特定约束从方法(i)导出。方法(iii)不严格，与正则分布的偏差为N^{-1/2}量级（N为粒子数）。恒温-恒压系综的结果与正则系综情况类似。
**canonical ensemble**: 正则系综，统计力学中具有固定粒子数、体积和温度的系统集合。
**equilibrium distribution functions**: 平衡分布函数，描述系统处于平衡态时微观状态概率分布的函数。
</code></pre>


> > 
> > 注意：术语解释在首次出现时添加。这里 "canonical ensemble" 首次出现，所以添加解释。还有 "equilibrium distribution functions" 也是核心术语，添加解释。但 "constant temperature molecular dynamics" 也是核心术语，可以在前面标题处添加。标题单元我们也可以添加术语解释。例如在文章标题单元后添加 `**constant temperature molecular dynamics methods**: 恒温分子动力学方法，一种模拟中保持温度恒定的分子动力学技术。` 但标题单元格式是 `# 英文标题` 然后中文标题，然后术语解释。那么我们在标题单元后添加术语解释。
> > 
> > 现在处理节标题和正文。
> > 
> > I. INTRODUCTION
> > 
> > 

<pre class="hljs"><code>I. INTRODUCTION
I. 引言
</code></pre>


> > 
> > 然后段落：
> > 
> > 

<pre class="hljs"><code>Recently, the extension of molecular dynamics (MD) methods to treat ensembles other than the traditional microcanonical ensemble has attracted considerable attention. The constant pressure MD method, first introduced by AndersenI and subsequently extended by Parrinello and
Rahman2-4 to allow for changes of the MD cell shape, has demonstrated its usefulness in applications to structural
changes in the solid state.2-9 Recently, Heyes lO employed a similar approach to that of Anderson but used real variables
instead of the scaled variables. l-4 A constant pressure MD method based on nonequilibrium MD technique was also proposed by Hoover et al. II Several constant temperature MD methods have been proposed. The purpose of the present article is to examine and compare these methods and to establish a unified formalism for their derivation. Emphasis is placed on the static properties and on the equilibrium distribution function. The earliest method for the constant temperature MD is a momentum scaling procedure, in which the velocities of the particles are scaled at each time step to maintain the total kinetic energy at a constant value. 12 This method has been used without demonstrated justification. Haile and Gupta13 discussed how to add the constraint ofconstant kinetic energy to the equations of motion. As a special case, they proposed a constraint method based on a momentum scaling procedure. This is a refinement of the earlier method. It will be shown in Sec. III C that the equilibrium distribution function in the momentum scaling method deviates from the canonical distribution by order N - 1/2 (N the number of particles).
最近，对分子动力学（MD）方法进行扩展，以处理传统微正则系综以外的系综，引起了相当大的关注。由AndersenI首次引入，随后由Parrinello和Rahman2-4扩展以允许MD胞形状变化的恒压MD方法，已在固态结构变化的应用中证明了其有效性。2-9最近，Heyes10采用了与Andersen类似的方法，但使用了实变量而非标度变量。1-4Hoover等人11还提出了基于非平衡MD技术的恒压MD方法。已经提出了几种恒温MD方法。本文的目的是检验和比较这些方法，并为其推导建立统一的表述。重点放在静态性质和平衡分布函数上。最早的恒温MD方法是动量标度法，其中在每个时间步对粒子速度进行标度，以保持总动能恒定。12该方法的使用缺乏证明。Haile和Gupta13讨论了如何将恒定动能约束添加到运动方程中。作为一种特殊情况，他们提出了一种基于动量标度步骤的约束方法。这是对早期方法的改进。将在第III C节中证明，动量标度法中的平衡分布函数与正则分布的偏差为N^{-1/2}量级（N为粒子数）。
**microcanonical ensemble**: 微正则系综，统计力学中具有固定粒子数、体积和能量的系统集合。
**constant pressure MD method**: 恒压分子动力学方法，模拟中保持压力恒定的分子动力学方法。
**momentum scaling**: 动量标度，通过调整粒子速度以控制温度的方法。
</code></pre>


> > 
> > 继续下一段：
> > 
> > 

<pre class="hljs"><code>Andersonl proposed a hybrid ofMD and Monte Carlo methods. In his approach, the particles change their velocities by stochastic collisions. The distribution of the velocities of the particles that collided is chosen to reproduce the canonical ensemble. Because of the sudden change of the velocities by collisions, the trajectory in the phase space is discontinuous.
Andersen1提出了MD与蒙特卡洛方法的混合方法。在他的方法中，粒子通过随机碰撞改变速度。碰撞粒子的速度分布选择为使正则系综再现。由于碰撞导致速度突变，相空间中的轨迹不连续。
</code></pre>


> > 
> > 然后脚注：
> > 
> >

-) 现地址：日本庆应义塾大学理工学部物理学科，横滨市港北区日吉3-14-1，邮编223。
</code></pre>

This method can produce the canonical distribution in coordinate space if we set g = 3N - 1, where N is the number of particles (in the original papers14.16g = 3N). Further, in Sec. III B, it will be shown that the HLME equations are derived from the extended system (ES) method 19 by imposing a particular constraint.
Hoover等人14,15和Evans16提出了一种约束MD方法，该方法源自非平衡MD表述。17,18此后该方法将被称为HLME方法。在该方法中，在力的项上增加了一个附加项-aPi于方程(1.2)中（qi为坐标；pi为粒子i的动量），
dqi/dt = pi/mi, (1.1)
dpi/dt = -∂φ/∂qi - a pi. (1.2)
因此，方程不再具有正则形式。参数a由总动能恒定的要求确定，
Σ pi·dpi/dt / mi = 0,
或

**extended system method**: 扩展系统方法，通过引入额外的自由度来模拟恒温条件的方法。
</code></pre>

<pre class="hljs"><code>The extended system method by Nosel9 introduced an additional degree of freedom s, which acts as an external system for the physical system ofN particles. Ifwe choose an appropriate potential gkT In s, for the variable s, the equilibrium distribution function, projected onto the physical system from the extended system of the particles and the variable s, is exactly that of the canonical ensemble. The parameter g is an integer, essentially equal to the number of degrees of freedom in the physical system, but the exact value depends on the particular procedure.
Nosé19提出的扩展系统方法引入了一个额外的自由度s，它作为N粒子物理系统的外部系统。如果我们为变量s选择合适的势gkT ln s，则从粒子和变量s的扩展系统投影到物理系统上的平衡分布函数恰好是正则系综的分布。参数g是一个整数，本质上等于物理系统的自由度数目，但确切值取决于具体过程。
</code></pre>

<pre class="hljs"><code>In the present article, the basic formalism is that of the extended system method and other methods are analyzed in this formulation. The details of the ES method are given in Sec. II. The equations of motion of the ES method with a constraint and the derivation of the HLME method are given in Sec. III. Extension to the constant temperature-constant pressure (TP) ensemble is discussed in Sec. IV. The method by Evans and Morriss 18 is derived from the ES meth
Od l9 in a similar fashion as for the canonical ensemble case. Most of the discussion is exact only if we ignore the momentum and the angular momentum conservation laws. The correction for the momentum conservation as well as other comments are given in Sec. V.
在本文中，基本形式为扩展系统方法，其他方法在此表述下进行分析。ES方法的细节在第II节中给出。具有约束的ES方法的运动方程以及HLME方法的推导在第III节中给出。第IV节讨论了向恒温-恒压（TP）系综的扩展。Evans和Morriss18的方法以与正则系综情况类似的方式从ES方法19导出。大多数讨论仅在忽略动量和角动量守恒定律时才是精确的。动量守恒的修正以及其他评论在第V节中给出。
</code></pre>


> > 
> > 然后节标题 II. THE EXTENDED SYSTEM METHOD
> > 
> > 

<pre class="hljs"><code>II. THE EXTENDED SYSTEM METHOD
II. 扩展系统方法
</code></pre>


> > 
> > 子节 A. A virtual variable formulation
> > 
> > 

<pre class="hljs"><code>A. A virtual variable formulation
A. 虚拟变量表述
</code></pre>


> > 
> > 然后段落：
> > 
> > 

<pre class="hljs"><code>We start from a method which seems to be most convenient to obtain the equilibrium distribution function. In the ES method,19 an additional degree of freedom s is introduced which acts as an external system on the physical system ofN particles, with coordinates q;, masses m; and poten
tial energy rp (q'). We also introduce virtual variables (coordinate q;. momentum Pi' and time t) which are related to the real variables (q;,p; ,t ') by
q; = q;. (2.1)
P; = p;ls, (2.2)
t' = r~t. (2.3)
The real velocity (dq;I dt ;) is also expressed via a scaled form in the virtual variable formulation
dq; dq; dqi
-=S-=S-.
dt' dt dt (2.4)
Thus, a simple interpretation of these transformations is scaling the time by dt' = dt Is. This is similar to scaling the coordinates in the constant pressure MD method. 1-4 The Hamiltonian ofthe extended system ofthe particles and the variable s in terms of the virtual variables is postulated as
H = ~)7/2mis2 + rp (q) + p;/2Q + gkT In s, (2.5)
i
Ps is the conjugate momentum of s; Q is a parameter of.dimension energy·(time)2 and behaves as a mass for the motion ofs· k is Boltzmann's constant; T the externally set tempera
tur~; the parameter g is essent~ally equal to the num?er of degrees of freedom of the phYSIcal system. However, ItS exact value will be chosen to satisfy the canonical distribution exactly at equilibrium. As we will discuss later, a logarithmic dependence of the potential on the variable s, gkT In s, is essential for producing the canonical ensemble. We assume the Hamiltonian formalism can be applied to Eq. (2.5) with the virtual variables. The equations ofmotion are
我们从一个似乎最方便获得平衡分布函数的方法开始。在ES方法中，19引入了一个额外的自由度s，它作为外部系统作用于N个粒子的物理系统，坐标为q_i，质量为m_i，势能为φ(q)。我们还引入虚拟变量（坐标q_i，动量p_i，时间t），它们与实变量（q_i', p_i', t'）的关系为
q_i = q_i', (2.1)
p_i = p_i'/s, (2.2)
t' = ∫ dt/s. (2.3)
真实速度(dq_i'/dt')在虚拟变量表述中也通过标度形式表示
dq_i'/dt' = s dq_i/dt = s dq_i/dt. (2.4)
因此，这些变换的一个简单解释是通过dt' = dt/s对时间进行标度。这类似于恒压MD方法中对坐标的标度。1-4以虚拟变量表示的粒子与变量s的扩展系统的哈密顿量假设为
H = Σ p_i^2/(2m_i s^2) + φ(q) + p_s^2/(2Q) + gkT ln s, (2.5)
p_s是s的共轭动量；Q是一个具有能量·(时间)^2量纲的参数，类似于s运动的质量；k是玻尔兹曼常数；T是外部设定的温度；参数g本质上等于物理系统的自由度数。然而，其确切值将选择为使平衡时的正则分布严格满足。我们将在后面讨论，势对变量s的对数依赖关系gkT ln s对于产生正则系综至关重要。我们假设哈密顿形式可以应用于具有虚拟变量的方程(2.5)。运动方程为
**Hamiltonian**: 哈密顿量，系统总能量的表示，通常为动能与势能之和。
**Boltzmann's constant**: 玻尔兹曼常数，将温度与能量联系起来的物理常数。
</code></pre>

<pre class="hljs"><code>dq_i/dt = ∂H/∂p_i = p_i/(m_i s^2), (2.6)
dp_i/dt = -∂H/∂q_i = -∂φ/∂q_i, (2.7)
ds/dt = ∂H/∂p_s = p_s/Q, (2.8)
dp_s/dt = -∂H/∂s = (Σ p_i^2/(m_i s^2) - gkT)/s. (2.9)
dq_i/dt = ∂H/∂p_i = p_i/(m_i s^2), (2.6)
dp_i/dt = -∂H/∂q_i = -∂φ/∂q_i, (2.7)
ds/dt = ∂H/∂p_s = p_s/Q, (2.8)
dp_s/dt = -∂H/∂s = (Σ p_i^2/(m_i s^2) - gkT)/s. (2.9)
</code></pre>

s d/dt (m_i s dq_i/dt) = -∂φ/∂q_i (2.11)

d/dt (Q ds/dt) = [Σ m_i (dq_i/dt)^2 - gkT]/s. (2.12)
</code></pre>

dH/dt = Σ_i (∂H/∂p_i dp_i/dt + ∂H/∂q_i dq_i/dt) + ∂H/∂p_s dp_s/dt + ∂H/∂s ds/dt = 0.

p(x1) = ∫ dx2 p(x1,x2).

<pre class="hljs"><code>In the extended system, the total Hamiltonian of Eq. (2.5) is conserved. Therefore, this method produces a microcanonical ensemble and the distribution function p(p,q,ps,s) is expressed as 8(H-E); 8(x) is the Dirac delta function. xexp [ _ (3Ng+ 1 )Ho(P',q')/kT].
If we choose g = 3N + 1, the partition function of the extended system is equivalent to that of the physical system in the canonical ensemble except for a constant factor:
Z = Cf dPJdq' exp[ - Ho(p',q')/kT],
and the equilibrium distribution function is
p(p',q') = exp[ - Ho(p',q')/kT]. (2.16)
在扩展系统中，方程(2.5)的总哈密顿量是守恒的。因此，该方法产生微正则系综，分布函数p(p,q,p_s,s)表示为δ(H-E)；δ(x)是狄拉克δ函数。简写形式dp = dp1 dp2 ... dpN, dq = dq1 dq2 ... dqN，以及H0(p,q) = Σ_i p_i^2/(2m_i) + φ(q)将被使用。配分函数为
Z = ∫ dp_s ∫ ds ∫ dp ∫ dq δ[ H0(p/s,q) + p_s^2/(2Q) + gkT ln s - E ]. (2.13)
虚拟动量p_i和坐标q_i变换为实变量p_i' = p_i/s, q_i' = q_i。体积元为dp dq = s^{3N} dp' dq'。因此
Z = ∫ dp_s ∫ dp' dq' ds s^{3N} δ[ H0(p',q') + p_s^2/(2Q) + gkT ln s - E ]. (2.14)
由于上述方程中δ函数的自变量作为变量s的函数只有一个零点，我们可以使用等价关系δ[f(s)] = δ(s - s0)/|f'(s0)|；s0是f(s)的零点。
Z = (1/(gkT)) ∫ dp_s ∫ dp' dq' ds s^{3N+1}
× δ(s - exp{ -[H0(p',q') + p_s^2/(2Q) - E]/(gkT)}) (2.15)
= (1/(gkT)) exp[(3N+1)E/(gkT)] ∫ dp_s
× exp[ -(3N+1)p_s^2/(2QgkT)] ∫ dp' dq'
× exp[ -(3N+1)H0(p',q')/(gkT)].
如果选择g = 3N + 1，扩展系统的配分函数与物理系统在正则系综中的配分函数等价，仅差一个常数因子：
Z = C ∫ dp' dq' exp[ -H0(p',q')/kT],
平衡分布函数为
p(p',q') = exp[ -H0(p',q')/kT]. (2.16)
**Dirac delta function**: 狄拉克δ函数，在除零点外处处为零，且积分为1的广义函数。
</code></pre>

<pre class="hljs"><code>With the quasiergodic hypothesis which relates the time average along the trajectory to the ensemble average, the averages of any static quantities expressed as functions of pJs,qj along the trajectory determined by Eqs. (2.6)-(2.9), are exactly those in the canonical ensemble:
1ito

(".) and (".)c denote the ensemble average in the extended system and in the canonical ensemble, respectively. The first equivalence in Eq. (2.17) is achieved by sampling data points at integer mUltiples of the virtual time unit ..1 t. We call this virtual time sampling. In this sampling, the real time interval of each time step is unequal. If we sample using equal intervals in real time t ' with tI = ff)dt Is (we can use an interpola
tion or the method in Sec. II B for this purpose), the result is a weighted average
lim - A (pls,q)dt' = lim -2.. - A (pls,q)

From comparison with Eq. (2.15), we find that if we chooseg as g = 3N, the weighted average in the extended system [Eq. (2.18)] is identical to (A (p',q') c' In virtual time sampling, g
should be 3N + 1, and in real time sampling, g must be 3N.
根据将沿轨迹的时间平均与系综平均联系起来的准各态历经假说，由方程(2.6)-(2.9)确定的轨迹上，任何表示为p_i/s, q_i的函数的静态量的平均值，恰好等于正则系综中的平均值：
lim_{t0→∞} (1/t0) ∫_0^{t0} A(p/s,q) dt = ⟨A(p/s,q)⟩ = ⟨A(p',q')⟩_c. (2.17)
⟨...⟩和⟨...⟩_c分别表示扩展系统和正则系综中的系综平均。方程(2.17)中的第一个等式是通过在虚拟时间单位Δt的整数倍处采样数据点来实现的。我们称之为虚拟时间采样。在这种采样中，每个时间步的真实时间间隔是不等的。如果我们使用真实时间t'中的等间隔采样，且t' = ∫ dt/s（我们可以使用插值或第II B节中的方法），结果是一个加权平均：
lim_{t0'→∞} (1/t0') ∫_0^{t0'} A(p/s,q) dt' = lim_{t0→∞} (1/t0) ∫_0^{t0} A(p/s,q) (dt'/s)
= [lim_{t0→∞} (1/t0) ∫_0^{t0} A(p/s,q)/s dt] / [lim_{t0→∞} (1/t0) ∫_0^{t0} dt/s]
= ⟨A(p/s,q)/s⟩ / ⟨1/s⟩. (2.18)
与方程(2.15)比较，我们发现如果选择g = 3N，扩展系统中的加权平均[方程(2.18)]与⟨A(p',q')⟩_c相同。在虚拟时间采样中，g应为3N+1；在真实时间采样中，g必须为3N。
**quasiergodic hypothesis**: 准各态历经假说，假设系统在长时间内将遍历所有可达微观状态，使得时间平均等于系综平均。
</code></pre>

<pre class="hljs"><code>We can construct another class ofconstant temperature MD method by replacing the potential for s by (for example)
gkTsn [n &gt; 0, an integer andg = (3N - n + l)1n]. The equilibrium distribution function can be readily obtained in a similar way.
p(p',q',ps) = ([ E - p;12Q - Ho(p',q')]/gkTjg
Xh [E-p;12Q-Ho(p',q')], (2.27)
where h (x) is the Heaviside function, h (x) = 1 for x&gt; 0, and
h (x) = °for x &lt; 0. The Heaviside function is necessary to
limit the range of the intergration in phase space. This constraint arises because the potential gkTsn is positive semidefinite; for some region in phase space (p',q',ps), the equation H = E does not have any real solution for s. With the most favorable assumption (sn) = 1, the val
ue of total energy E = (Ho(p/s,q) + (p;12Q) + gkT. Us
ing the definition HI = (Ho(p/s,q) - Ho(p',q') + (p;12Q) - p;12Q, Eq. (2.27) is
p(p',q',p.) = (1 + H/gkT)gh (gkT + HI)'
Note that the distribution for the variablePs cannot be separated from those of p' and q'. From the expansion of
g In(1 + a/g) with respect to lIg; g In(l + a/g) = a - a2/
2g + a3/3i ..., p is approximated as
p = exp[H/kT - ~(HI/kTf/g]. (2.28)
Singe H I is a quantity of order N 1/2, the leading term of the deviation of p from the canonical distribution is of order N-1I2.
我们可以通过将s的势替换为（例如）gkT s^n [n &gt; 0，整数，且g = (3N - n + 1)/n]来构造另一类恒温MD方法。平衡分布函数可以用类似方法轻易获得。
p(p',q',p_s) = ([E - p_s^2/(2Q) - H0(p',q')]/(gkT))^g h[E - p_s^2/(2Q) - H0(p',q')], (2.27)
其中h(x)是赫维赛德函数，对于x&gt;0，h(x)=1；对于x&lt;0，h(x)=0。赫维赛德函数是必要的，以限制相空间中的积分范围。这一约束源于势gkT s^n是正半定的；对于相空间中的某些区域(p',q',p_s)，方程H = E对s没有任何实数解。在最有利的假设⟨s^n⟩ = 1下，总能量E = ⟨H0(p/s,q)⟩ + ⟨p_s^2/(2Q)⟩ + gkT。利用定义H1 = H0(p/s,q) - H0(p',q') + p_s^2/(2Q) - p_s^2/(2Q)，方程(2.27)变为
p(p',q',p_s) = (1 + H1/(gkT))^g h(gkT + H1).
注意，变量p_s的分布不能与p'和q'的分布分离。从g ln(1 + a/g)关于1/g的展开：g ln(1 + a/g) = a - a^2/(2g) + a^3/(3g^2) - ...，p近似为
p = exp[ H1/kT - (H1/kT)^2/(2g) ]. (2.28)
由于H1是一个N^{1/2}量级的量，p与正则分布的偏差的主要项为N^{-1/2}量级。
**Heaviside function**: 赫维赛德函数，即单位阶跃函数，在正半轴为1，负半轴为0。
</code></pre>

<pre class="hljs"><code>As we can see from the derivation of the equilibrium distribution function [see Eqs. (2.14) and (2.15)], it is related to the inverse function of the potential for s.
p(p',q',p.) = f dS'S3N8[H" +I(s) - E]
= f dS'S3N8(s - so)ll'(so) = S6N /I'(so);
So satisfies the relation I(so) = E - H", thus So =I-I (E - H "). H" is that part ofthe Hamiltonian H [Eq. (2.5)] which is independent of s. Therefore, a logarithmic form is essential to produce the canonical distribution.
从平衡分布函数的推导[见方程(2.14)和(2.15)]可以看出，它与s的势的反函数相关。
p(p',q',p_s) = ∫ ds s^{3N} δ[H'' + f(s) - E] = ∫ ds s^{3N} δ(s - s0)/|f'(s0)| = s0^{3N} / |f'(s0)|;
s0满足关系f(s0) = E - H''，因此s0 = f^{-1}(E - H'')。H''是哈密顿量H[方程(2.5)]中与s无关的部分。因此，对数形式对于产生正则分布至关重要。
</code></pre>

如果设参数α = ds/dt，方程(3.8)-(3.10)与HLME方法14-18的方程(1.1)、(1.2)和(1.5)相同。这些方程基于实时采样，因此如果设g = 3N - 1，我们恢复正则分布。在原始文献14,16中，g = 3N被使用。如上所示，HLME方法等价于带有附加约束方程(3.2)的ES方法。
</code></pre>

x~ {s - [f(p;lm;)/gkT ]l}.

除方程(2.5)中使用的对数形式外，任何其他对s的势函数的选择都会导致与正则系综不同的系综。作为一个例子，我们推导Haile和Gupta13的动量标度（HG）方法的改进形式的平衡分布函数。
起始哈密顿量为（s对应参考文献13中的1-s）
H = Σ_i p_i^2/(2m_i s) + φ(q) + (1/2) g k T s. (3.12)
约束条件为
∂H/∂s = - Σ_i p_i^2/(2m_i s^2) + (1/2) g k T = 0, (3.13)
或

Z = [ ∫ dp' δ( Σ_i p_i'^2/(2m_i) - g k T/2 ) ] ∫ dq ∫ ds s^{3N-1}
× δ[ g k T s + φ(q) - E ]
= [ ∫ dp' δ( Σ_i p_i'^2/(2m_i) - g k T/2 ) ] ∫ dq
× { [E - φ(q)]/(g k T) }^{3N-1} h[E - φ(q)]. (3.16)

× ( 1 + [⟨φ⟩ - φ(q)]/(g k T) )^g

<pre class="hljs"><code>In the same way as in Sec. II C, Eq. (3.17} deviates from exp[ - &lt;p (q)/kT] by orderN- I. The coordinate part ofp(p',q) in Eq. (3.17) is similar to the equilibrium distribution function of the microcanonical ensemble projected onto coordinate Space,
Pmc(q) = J dp c5(fP7/2m; + t/J - E)
= jdPc5(P 2 +t/J -E)JdP8[P- (fP;/2m;)I]
= CfdP.P3N-18lP2 + t/J-E)
= C'JdP·p 3N - 2c5[P_ (E - &lt;p JI]
33
=C'(E_t/J)2N- 1 =C"(l + (t/J) -t/J)2N- 1
3NkTI2 '
(3.18)
where P is a radius of a 3N dimension sphere and
E = VVkT + (; ). Therefore, the difference between the canonical ensemble and the ensemble generated by the HG method13 is the same order as we expect between the microcanonical and the canonical ensembles. The ensemble cor
rections of order N -I for first order quantities (energy, virial, ...) and those of order one for quantities relating to fluctuation formulas (heat capacity, compressibility, ...J are generally expected.21•22 For example, the fluctuatioll of the potenti~ energy in the HG method is
«(8&lt;P )2) = N(kT)23c:/(3 + c:) + 0(1), (3.19)
where c: = (liNk )(d (t/J )ldT) is a heat capacity ofthe coordinate part.
与第II C节相同的方式，方程(3.17)与exp[-φ(q)/kT]的偏差为N^{-1}量级。方程(3.17)中p(p',q)的坐标部分类似于微正则系综投影到坐标空间的平衡分布函数，
P_mc(q) = ∫ dp δ( Σ_i p_i^2/(2m_i) + φ - E )
= ∫ dP δ(P^2 + φ - E) ∫ dP δ[P - ( Σ_i p_i^2/(2m_i) )^{1/2} ]
= C ∫ dP P^{3N-1} δ(P^2 + φ - E)
= C' ∫ dP P^{3N-2} δ[P - (E - φ)^{1/2}]
= C' (E - φ)^{(3N-2)/2} = C'' ( 1 + (⟨φ⟩ - φ)/(3N k T/2) )^{(3N-2)/2}, (3.18)
其中P是3N维球体的半径，且E = (3N/2)kT + ⟨φ⟩。因此，正则系综与HG方法13产生的系综之间的差异，与我们预期微正则系综与正则系综之间的差异具有相同的量级。对于一阶量（能量、维里等），通常预期N^{-1}量级的系综修正；对于与涨落公式相关的量（热容、压缩率等），预期量级为1的修正。21,22例如，HG方法中势能的涨落为
⟨(δφ)^2⟩ = N (kT)^2 3c/(3+c) + O(1), (3.19)
其中c = (1/Nk)(d⟨φ⟩/dT)是坐标部分的热容。
**virial**: 维里，与粒子间作用力与位置乘积相关的量，常用于计算压力。
</code></pre>

<pre class="hljs"><code>Combined with the constant pressure MD method of Anderson,1 the canonical ensemble MD method can be readily extended to the TP ensemble. 19 Here we use the formulation for uniform dilation given by Anderson,1 but the extension to the generalized form of the constant pressure simulation method by Parrinello and Rahman can be derived in a similar way. 2--4 In the TP ensemble, the virtual variables (qop;, s, V, t) are related to the real variables (q;,p;, s, V, 1') via scaling of the coordinates by VI /3 and scaling of the time by s (V, the volume of a MD cell),

结合Andersen1的恒压MD方法，正则系综MD方法可以容易地扩展至TP系综。19这里我们使用Andersen给出的均匀膨胀公式，但向Parrinello和Rahman2-4的广义恒压模拟方法的扩展可以类似方式推导。在TP系综中，虚拟变量(q_i, p_i, s, V, t)通过坐标乘以V^{1/3}和时间乘以s进行标度，与实变量(q_i', p_i', s, V, t')相关联（V为MD胞的体积）：
q_i = V^{1/3} q_i', (4.1)
p_i = p_i' / (V^{1/3} s), (4.2)
t' = ∫ dt / s. (4.3)
标度坐标q_i'的各分量值限制在0到1的范围内。哈密顿量为
H = Σ_i p_i^2/(2m_i V^{2/3} s^2) + φ(V^{1/3} q') + p_s^2/(2Q) + gkT ln s + p_v^2/(2W) + P_ex V, (4.4)
其中p_v是V的共轭动量，W是体积运动的“质量”，P_ex是外部设定的压力。运动方程为
dq_i/dt = ∂H/∂p_i = p_i/(m_i V^{2/3} s^2), (4.5)
dp_i/dt = -∂H/∂q_i = -∂φ/∂q_i, (4.6)
ds/dt = ∂H/∂p_s = p_s/Q, (4.7)
dp_s/dt = -∂H/∂s = [ Σ_i p_i^2/(m_i V^{2/3} s^2) - gkT ] / s, (4.8)
dV/dt = -∂H/∂p_v = p_v/W, (4.9)
dp_v/dt = -∂H/∂V = [ Σ_i p_i^2/(m_i V^{2/3} s^2) - ∂φ/∂q_i' · q_i' ] / (3V) - P_ex. (4.10)

<pre class="hljs"><code>V. DISCUSSION
V. 讨论
</code></pre>


> > 
> > A. Comparison of the extended system method and the constraint method
> > 
> > 

<pre class="hljs"><code>A. Comparison of the extended system method and the constraint method
A. 扩展系统方法与约束方法的比较
</code></pre>


> > 
> > 段落：
> > 
> > 

<pre class="hljs"><code>The constant temperature MD methods for the canonical and the TP ensembles are reviewed in Secs. II-IV. The
用于正则系综和TP系综的恒温MD方法在第II-IV节中进行了回顾。
</code></pre>


> > 
> > 然后表格 TABLE I：
> > 
> > 

<pre class="hljs"><code>TABLE I. Relation between the various constant temperature methods.
Extended system method
relation of these equations is listed in Table 1. The HLME method 14-18 and Evans-Morriss method 18 were derived as a special case ofthe ES method. 19 Other methods except those mentioned here and that by Anderson I do not seem to give the rigorous canonical distribution. The methods were first presented in the virtual variable formulation, then transformed to the real variable case. The virtual variable formulation is the backbone of the constant temperature method. In this form, the equations remain canonical and the proof of equivalence with the canonical ensemble is straightforward. However, the unequal time intervals are not convenient for simulations. The real time formulation is recommended for applications. It should be remembered that in this case the equations are no longer canonical. Both constant temperature methods (the ES and the HLME) are still not ideal. The number ofindependent variables are listed in Table II. The ES method has more independent variables than the equivalent statistical mechanical ensemble. This is the reason why the ES method give$ correct results for the static quantities, but the time evolution of s
andlor V are dependent on the adjustable parameters Q andlor W. This arbitrariness is both a disadvantage and an advantage of this method. The calculation of the velocity autocorrelation functions in the constant pressure MD method7 and in the constant temperature method 13.24.25 show no significant difference from that of the ordinary MD method. Ifthe effects on the dynamics of the physical system
are negligible, we can select the parameters Qand W to optimize the efficiency of the calculations. In the constraint method, the number of independent variables is less than those of the statistical mechanical formulation, due to the addition ofthe constraints. Some of the static quantities in the constraint method are not exactly those appropriate to the canonical distribution. In the canonical ensemble, only the quantities dependent on the momentum are affected. In the TP ensemble, the pressure constaint depends both on the volume and on coordinate space. Ifwe define the instantaneous temperature Ti and pressure Pi by
and
表I. 各种恒温方法之间的关系。
扩展系统方法
这些方程的关系列于表I。HLME方法14-18和Evans-Morriss方法18作为ES方法19的特例被导出。除此处提及的方法和Andersen1的方法外，其他方法似乎都不能给出严格的正则分布。这些方法首先在虚拟变量表述中提出，然后转换到实变量情形。虚拟变量表述是恒温方法的支柱。在这种形式中，方程保持正则，与正则系综等价的证明是直接的。然而，不等的时间间隔在模拟中不方便。实变量表述推荐用于应用。应记住，在这种情况下方程不再是正则的。两种恒温方法（ES和HLME）仍不理想。独立变量的数目列于表II。ES方法比等效的统计力学系综具有更多的独立变量。这就是ES方法对静态量给出正确结果的原因，但s和/或V的时间演化依赖于可调参数Q和/或W。这种任意性既是该方法的缺点也是优点。恒压MD方法7和恒温方法13,24,25中速度自相关函数的计算结果显示与普通MD方法无显著差异。如果对物理系统动力学的影响可忽略，我们可以选择参数Q和W以优化计算效率。在约束方法中，由于添加了约束，独立变量的数目少于统计力学表述中的数目。约束方法中的某些静态量并非恰好对应于正则分布。在正则系综中，只有依赖于动量的量受到影响。在TP系综中，压力约束同时依赖于体积和坐标空间。如果我们定义瞬时温度T_i和压力P_i为
</code></pre>

> > 
> > 然后 VI. SUMMARY
> > 
> > 

<pre class="hljs"><code>VI. SUMMARY
VI. 总结
</code></pre>


> > 
> > 段落：
> > 
> > 

<pre class="hljs"><code>Three constant temperature MD methods are examined analytically. Except for effects due to momentum and angular momentum conservation, the ES method19 gives rigorous equilibrium distribution functions in the canonical and in the TP ensembles. The HLME constraint method 14-18 give the canonical distribution only in coordinate space. Both the HLME and the Evans-Morriss method have been derived from the formulation of the ES method by imposing constraints. The Haile-Gupta method 13 on the other hand does not give the rigorous canonical distribution.
The virtual variable formulation is best suited for proof ofthe equivalence with the statistical mechanical ensembles. The equations based on the real variable formulations [Eqs. (2.19)-(2.25) or the HLME method] are recommended for applications. The extension of the MD method to ensembles other than the microcanonical ensemble is formulated in a unified fashion. By introducing real variables and virtual variables the constant pressure MD method is generated from a scaling ofthe coordinates. The constant temperature MD method is obtained from a scaling of the time.
对三种恒温MD方法进行了分析检验。除动量和角动量守恒的影响外，ES方法19在正则系综和TP系综中给出了严格的平衡分布函数。HLME约束方法14-18仅在坐标空间中给出正则分布。HLME方法和Evans-Morriss方法均通过施加约束从ES方法的表述导出。另一方面，Haile-Gupta方法13不给出严格的正则分布。
虚拟变量表述最适合于证明与统计力学系综的等价性。基于实变量表述的方程[方程(2.19)-(2.25)或HLME方法]推荐用于应用。将MD方法扩展到微正则系综以外的系综以统一的方式表述。通过引入实变量和虚拟变量，恒压MD方法由坐标的标度生成。恒温MD方法由时间的标度得到。
</code></pre>

<span class="hljs-section"># A unified formulation of the constant temperature molecular dynamics methods</span>
恒温分子动力学方法的统一表述
<span class="hljs-strong">**constant temperature molecular dynamics methods**</span>: 恒温分子动力学方法，在模拟过程中保持温度恒定的一类分子动力学技术。

Shuichi Nosea)
Division o/Chemistry. National Research Councilo/Canada. Ottawa. Ontario. Canada. K1A OR6
(Received 17 November 1983; accepted 15 March 1984)
Shuichi Nosea)
加拿大国家研究院化学分部，渥太华，安大略省，加拿大，K1A OR6
（接收日期：1983年11月17日；接受日期：1984年3月15日）

Three recently proposed constant temperature molecular dynamics methods by: (i) Nose (Mol. Phys., to be published); (ii) Hoover et al. [Phys. Rev. Lett. 48, 1818 (1982)], and Evans and Morriss [Chem. Phys. 77, 63 (1983)]; and (iii) Haile and Gupta [J. Chem. Phys. 79, 3067 (1983)] are examined analytically via calculating the equilibrium distribution functions and comparing them with that ofthe canonical ensemble. Except for effects due to momentum and angular momentum conservation, method (i) yields the rigorous canonical distribution in both momentum and coordinate space. Method (ii) can be made rigorous in coordinate space, and can be derived from method (i) by imposing a specific constraint. Method (iii) is not rigorous and gives a deviation of order N -1/2 from the canonical distribution (N the number of particles). The results for the constant temperature-constant pressure ensemble are similar to the canonical ensemble case.
最近提出的三种恒温分子动力学方法：(i) Nosé（Mol. Phys.，待发表）；(ii) Hoover等人[Phys. Rev. Lett. 48, 1818 (1982)]，以及Evans和Morriss[Chem. Phys. 77, 63 (1983)]；(iii) Haile和Gupta[J. Chem. Phys. 79, 3067 (1983)]，通过计算平衡分布函数并与正则系综进行比较，进行了分析检验。除动量和角动量守恒的影响外，方法(i)在动量空间和坐标空间中都给出了严格的正则分布。方法(ii)在坐标空间中可以做到严格，并可通过施加特定约束从方法(i)导出。方法(iii)不严格，与正则分布的偏差为N^{-1/2}量级（N为粒子数）。恒温-恒压系综的结果与正则系综情况类似。
<span class="hljs-strong">**canonical ensemble**</span>: 正则系综，统计力学中具有固定粒子数、体积和温度的系统集合。
<span class="hljs-strong">**equilibrium distribution functions**</span>: 平衡分布函数，描述系统处于平衡态时微观状态概率分布的函数。

<span class="hljs-section">## I. INTRODUCTION</span>
I. 引言

Recently, the extension of molecular dynamics (MD) methods to treat ensembles other than the traditional microcanonical ensemble has attracted considerable attention. The constant pressure MD method, first introduced by AndersenI and subsequently extended by Parrinello and
Rahman2-4 to allow for changes of the MD cell shape, has demonstrated its usefulness in applications to structural
changes in the solid state.2-9 Recently, Heyes lO employed a similar approach to that of Anderson but used real variables
instead of the scaled variables. l-4 A constant pressure MD method based on nonequilibrium MD technique was also proposed by Hoover et al. II Several constant temperature MD methods have been proposed. The purpose of the present article is to examine and compare these methods and to establish a unified formalism for their derivation. Emphasis is placed on the static properties and on the equilibrium distribution function. The earliest method for the constant temperature MD is a momentum scaling procedure, in which the velocities of the particles are scaled at each time step to maintain the total kinetic energy at a constant value. 12 This method has been used without demonstrated justification. Haile and Gupta13 discussed how to add the constraint ofconstant kinetic energy to the equations of motion. As a special case, they proposed a constraint method based on a momentum scaling procedure. This is a refinement of the earlier method. It will be shown in Sec. III C that the equilibrium distribution function in the momentum scaling method deviates from the canonical distribution by order N - 1/2 (N the number of particles).
最近，对分子动力学（MD）方法进行扩展，以处理传统微正则系综以外的系综，引起了相当大的关注。由Andersen1首次引入，随后由Parrinello和Rahman2-4扩展以允许MD胞形状变化的恒压MD方法，已在固态结构变化的应用中证明了其有效性。2-9最近，Heyes10采用了与Andersen类似的方法，但使用了实变量而非标度变量。1-4Hoover等人11还提出了基于非平衡MD技术的恒压MD方法。已经提出了几种恒温MD方法。本文的目的是检验和比较这些方法，并为其推导建立统一的表述。重点放在静态性质和平衡分布函数上。最早的恒温MD方法是动量标度法，其中在每个时间步对粒子速度进行标度，以保持总动能恒定。12该方法的使用缺乏证明。Haile和Gupta13讨论了如何将恒定动能约束添加到运动方程中。作为一种特殊情况，他们提出了一种基于动量标度步骤的约束方法。这是对早期方法的改进。将在第III C节中证明，动量标度法中的平衡分布函数与正则分布的偏差为N^{-1/2}量级（N为粒子数）。
<span class="hljs-strong">**microcanonical ensemble**</span>: 微正则系综，统计力学中具有固定粒子数、体积和能量的系统集合。
<span class="hljs-strong">**constant pressure MD method**</span>: 恒压分子动力学方法，模拟中保持压力恒定的分子动力学方法。
<span class="hljs-strong">**momentum scaling**</span>: 动量标度，通过调整粒子速度以控制温度的方法。

Andersonl proposed a hybrid ofMD and Monte Carlo methods. In his approach, the particles change their velocities by stochastic collisions. The distribution of the velocities of the particles that collided is chosen to reproduce the canonical ensemble. Because of the sudden change of the velocities by collisions, the trajectory in the phase space is discontinuous.
Andersen1提出了MD与蒙特卡洛方法的混合方法。在他的方法中，粒子通过随机碰撞改变速度。碰撞粒子的速度分布选择为使正则系综再现。由于碰撞导致速度突变，相空间中的轨迹不连续。

-) 现地址：日本庆应义塾大学理工学部物理学科，横滨市港北区日吉3-14-1，邮编223。

Hoover et al. 14•15 and Evans l6 proposed a constraint MD method which was derived from a nonequilibrium MD
formulation. 17.18 This method will be called the HLME method hereafter. In this method, an additional term - aPi is added to the force term in Eq. (1.2) (qi&gt; coordinate; Pi&gt; momentum of particle i), dqi

This method can produce the canonical distribution in coordinate space if we set g = 3N - 1, where N is the number of particles (in the original papers14.16g = 3N). Further, in Sec. III B, it will be shown that the HLME equations are derived from the extended system (ES) method 19 by imposing a particular constraint.
Hoover等人14,15和Evans16提出了一种约束MD方法，该方法源自非平衡MD表述。17,18此后该方法将被称为HLME方法。在该方法中，在力的项上增加了一个附加项-aPi于方程(1.2)中（qi为坐标；pi为粒子i的动量），
dqi/dt = pi/mi, (1.1)
dpi/dt = -∂φ/∂qi - a pi. (1.2)
因此，方程不再具有正则形式。参数a由总动能恒定的要求确定，
Σ pi·dpi/dt / mi = 0,
或
Σ pi·(-∂φ/∂qi) / mi - a Σ pi^2/mi = 0.
从而得到
a = (Σ pi·∂φ/∂qi / mi) / (Σ pi^2/mi). (1.5)
如果设g = 3N - 1，其中N为粒子数（原始文献14,16中g = 3N），该方法可在坐标空间中产生正则分布。此外，在第III B节中将证明，HLME方程是通过施加特定约束从扩展系统（ES）方法19导出的。
<span class="hljs-strong">**extended system method**</span>: 扩展系统方法，通过引入额外的自由度来模拟恒温条件的方法。

The extended system method by Nosel9 introduced an additional degree of freedom s, which acts as an external system for the physical system ofN particles. Ifwe choose an appropriate potential gkT In s, for the variable s, the equilibrium distribution function, projected onto the physical system from the extended system of the particles and the variable s, is exactly that of the canonical ensemble. The parameter g is an integer, essentially equal to the number of degrees of freedom in the physical system, but the exact value depends on the particular procedure.
Nosé19提出的扩展系统方法引入了一个额外的自由度s，它作为N粒子物理系统的外部系统。如果我们为变量s选择合适的势gkT ln s，则从粒子和变量s的扩展系统投影到物理系统上的平衡分布函数恰好是正则系综的分布。参数g是一个整数，本质上等于物理系统的自由度数目，但确切值取决于具体过程。

J. Chern. Phys. 81 (1). 1 July 1984 0021-9606/84/130511-09$02.10 © 1984 American Institute of Physics 511
J. Chem. Phys. 81 (1), 1984年7月1日 0021-9606/84/130511-09$02.10 © 1984 美国物理联合会 511

In the present article, the basic formalism is that of the extended system method and other methods are analyzed in this formulation. The details of the ES method are given in Sec. II. The equations of motion of the ES method with a constraint and the derivation of the HLME method are given in Sec. III. Extension to the constant temperature-constant pressure (TP) ensemble is discussed in Sec. IV. The method by Evans and Morriss 18 is derived from the ES meth
Od l9 in a similar fashion as for the canonical ensemble case. Most of the discussion is exact only if we ignore the momentum and the angular momentum conservation laws. The correction for the momentum conservation as well as other comments are given in Sec. V.
在本文中，基本形式为扩展系统方法，其他方法在此表述下进行分析。ES方法的细节在第II节中给出。具有约束的ES方法的运动方程以及HLME方法的推导在第III节中给出。第IV节讨论了向恒温-恒压（TP）系综的扩展。Evans和Morriss18的方法以与正则系综情况类似的方式从ES方法19导出。大多数讨论仅在忽略动量和角动量守恒定律时才是精确的。动量守恒的修正以及其他评论在第V节中给出。

<span class="hljs-section">## II. THE EXTENDED SYSTEM METHOD</span>
II. 扩展系统方法

<span class="hljs-section">### A. A virtual variable formulation</span>
A. 虚拟变量表述

tial energy rp (q'). We also introduce virtual variables (coordinate q;. momentum Pi' and time t) which are related to the real variables (q;,p; ,t ') by

守恒量为哈密顿量H、总动量Σ p<span class="hljs-emphasis">_i和角动量Σ q_</span>i × p<span class="hljs-emphasis">_i。
dH/dt = Σ_</span>i (∂H/∂p<span class="hljs-emphasis">_i dp_</span>i/dt + ∂H/∂q<span class="hljs-emphasis">_i dq_</span>i/dt) + ∂H/∂p<span class="hljs-emphasis">_s dp_</span>s/dt + ∂H/∂s ds/dt = 0.
后两个量的守恒定律可从方程(2.7)和势满足的性质导出
Σ<span class="hljs-emphasis">_i ∂φ/∂q_</span>i = 0
和
Σ<span class="hljs-emphasis">_i q_</span>i × ∂φ/∂q<span class="hljs-emphasis">_i = 0.
然而，应注意，在具有周期性边界条件的普通模拟中，角动量并不守恒。由于动量和角动量守恒，MD方法产生的系综与通常的统计力学系综略有不同。20-22这些微小的偏差在第II-IV节的讨论中被忽略。N个全同粒子的配分函数Z通过对平衡分布函数p(x1,x2,...)在整个相空间上进行积分获得。
Z = (1/(N! h^{3N})) ∫ dx1 dx2 ... p(x1,x2,...),
其中h是普朗克常数，xi是广义坐标（此后忽略p和Z的常数因子）。从空间(x1,x2)到空间(x1)的平衡分布函数投影通过关于变量x2的积分实现。
p(x1) = ∫ dx2 p(x1,x2).
特别是，我们需要一个从扩展系统投影到物理系统的分布函数p(p',q')。
<span class="hljs-strong">**partition function**</span>: 配分函数，统计力学中求和所有可能状态权重因子的函数，是联系微观与宏观的桥梁。
<span class="hljs-strong">**phase space**</span>: 相空间，由系统所有粒子的坐标和动量构成的抽象空间。

In the extended system, the total Hamiltonian of Eq. (2.5) is conserved. Therefore, this method produces a microcanonical ensemble and the distribution function p(p,q,ps,s) is expressed as 8(H-E); 8(x) is the Dirac delta function. The shortened forms dp = dp 1dp2".dpN' dq = dq1dq2".dqN' and
Ho(p,q) = ~jp7/2mj + ¢ (q) are used. The partition function is

If we choose g = 3N + 1, the partition function of the extended system is equivalent to that of the physical system in the canonical ensemble except for a constant factor:

and the equilibrium distribution function is

在扩展系统中，方程(2.5)的总哈密顿量是守恒的。因此，该方法产生微正则系综，分布函数p(p,q,p<span class="hljs-emphasis">_s,s)表示为δ(H-E)；δ(x)是狄拉克δ函数。简写形式dp = dp1 dp2 ... dpN, dq = dq1 dq2 ... dqN，以及H0(p,q) = Σ_</span>i p<span class="hljs-emphasis">_i^2/(2m_</span>i) + φ(q)将被使用。配分函数为

如果选择g = 3N + 1，扩展系统的配分函数与物理系统在正则系综中的配分函数等价，仅差一个常数因子：

<span class="hljs-strong">**Dirac delta function**</span>: 狄拉克δ函数，在除零点外处处为零，且积分为1的广义函数。

<span class="hljs-strong">**quasiergodic hypothesis**</span>: 准各态历经假说，假设系统在长时间内将遍历所有可达微观状态，使得时间平均等于系综平均。

<span class="hljs-section">### C. Other potentials for the variable s</span>
C. 变量s的其他势

We can construct another class ofconstant temperature MD method by replacing the potential for s by (for example)
gkTsn [n &gt; 0, an integer andg = (3N - n + l)1n]. The equilibrium distribution function can be readily obtained in a similar way.
p(p',q',ps) = ([ E - p;12Q - Ho(p',q')]/gkTjg
Xh [E-p;12Q-Ho(p',q')], (2.27)
where h (x) is the Heaviside function, h (x) = 1 for x&gt; 0, and
h (x) = °for x &lt; 0. The Heaviside function is necessary to
limit the range of the intergration in phase space. This constraint arises because the potential gkTsn is positive semidefinite; for some region in phase space (p',q',ps), the equation H = E does not have any real solution for s. With the most favorable assumption (sn) = 1, the val
ue of total energy E = (Ho(p/s,q) + (p;12Q) + gkT. Us
ing the definition HI = (Ho(p/s,q) - Ho(p',q') + (p;12Q) - p;12Q, Eq. (2.27) is
p(p',q',p.) = (1 + H/gkT)gh (gkT + HI)'
Note that the distribution for the variablePs cannot be separated from those of p' and q'. From the expansion of
g In(1 + a/g) with respect to lIg; g In(l + a/g) = a - a2/
2g + a3/3i ..., p is approximated as
p = exp[H/kT - ~(HI/kTf/g]. (2.28)
Singe H I is a quantity of order N 1/2, the leading term of the deviation of p from the canonical distribution is of order N-1I2.
我们可以通过将s的势替换为（例如）gkT s^n [n &gt; 0，整数，且g = (3N - n + 1)/n]来构造另一类恒温MD方法。平衡分布函数可以用类似方法轻易获得。
p(p',q',p<span class="hljs-emphasis">_s) = ([E - p_</span>s^2/(2Q) - H0(p',q')]/(gkT))^g h[E - p<span class="hljs-emphasis">_s^2/(2Q) - H0(p',q')], (2.27)
其中h(x)是赫维赛德函数，对于x&gt;0，h(x)=1；对于x&lt;0，h(x)=0。赫维赛德函数是必要的，以限制相空间中的积分范围。这一约束源于势gkT s^n是正半定的；对于相空间中的某些区域(p',q',p_</span>s)，方程H = E对s没有任何实数解。在最有利的假设⟨s^n⟩ = 1下，总能量E = ⟨H0(p/s,q)⟩ + ⟨p<span class="hljs-emphasis">_s^2/(2Q)⟩ + gkT。利用定义H1 = H0(p/s,q) - H0(p',q') + p_</span>s^2/(2Q) - p<span class="hljs-emphasis">_s^2/(2Q)，方程(2.27)变为
p(p',q',p_</span>s) = (1 + H1/(gkT))^g h(gkT + H1).
注意，变量p<span class="hljs-emphasis">_s的分布不能与p'和q'的分布分离。从g ln(1 + a/g)关于1/g的展开：g ln(1 + a/g) = a - a^2/(2g) + a^3/(3g^2) - ...，p近似为
p = exp[ H1/kT - (H1/kT)^2/(2g) ]. (2.28)
由于H1是一个N^{1/2}量级的量，p与正则分布的偏差的主要项为N^{-1/2}量级。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Heaviside function**</span>: 赫维赛德函数，即单位阶跃函数，在正半轴为1，负半轴为0。

As we can see from the derivation of the equilibrium distribution function [see Eqs. (2.14) and (2.15)], it is related to the inverse function of the potential for s.
p(p',q',p.) = f dS'S3N8[H" +I(s) - E]
= f dS'S3N8(s - so)ll'(so) = S6N /I'(so);
So satisfies the relation I(so) = E - H", thus So =I-I (E - H "). H" is that part ofthe Hamiltonian H [Eq. (2.5)] which is independent of s. Therefore, a logarithmic form is essential to produce the canonical distribution.
从平衡分布函数的推导[见方程(2.14)和(2.15)]可以看出，它与s的势的反函数相关。
p(p',q',p_</span>s) = ∫ ds s^{3N} δ[H'' + f(s) - E] = ∫ ds s^{3N} δ(s - s0)/|f'(s0)| = s0^{3N} / |f'(s0)|;
s0满足关系f(s0) = E - H''，因此s0 = f^{-1}(E - H'')。H''是哈密顿量H[方程(2.5)]中与s无关的部分。因此，对数形式对于产生正则分布至关重要。

<span class="hljs-section">## III. THE CONSTRAINT METHOD</span>
III. 约束方法

<span class="hljs-section">### A. Virtual variable formulation</span>
A. 虚拟变量表述

The distribution function in momentum space is usually simple and the contribution of this term can be easily calculated in the canonical ensemble. Therefore, any method that produces the canonical distribution, even if only in coordinate space, can be useful in some situations. The standard way for this approach is to constrain the total kinetic energy term

dH/dt = Σ_</span>i (∂H/∂p<span class="hljs-emphasis">_i dp_</span>i/dt + ∂H/∂q<span class="hljs-emphasis">_i dq_</span>i/dt) + ∂H/∂p<span class="hljs-emphasis">_s dp_</span>s/dt + ∂H/∂s ds/dt = 0.
此情况下的配分函数为[记得H0(p,q) = Σ<span class="hljs-emphasis">_i p_</span>i^2/(2m<span class="hljs-emphasis">_i) + φ(q)]
Z = ∫ ds ∫ dp ∫ dq δ[ H0(p/s,q) + gkT ln s - E ] δ{ s - [ Σ_</span>i p<span class="hljs-emphasis">_i^2/(m_</span>i g kT) ]^{1/2} }
= ∫ ds ∫ dp ∫ dq δ[ H0(p/s,q) + gkT ln s - E ] s g kT δ( s^2 g kT/2 - Σ<span class="hljs-emphasis">_i p_</span>i^2/(2m<span class="hljs-emphasis">_i) ).
通过变换p_</span>i' = p<span class="hljs-emphasis">_i/s, q_</span>i' = q<span class="hljs-emphasis">_i，我们得到
Z = [ ∫ dp' δ( Σ_</span>i p<span class="hljs-emphasis">_i'^2/(2m_</span>i) - g kT/2 ) ] ∫ dq' ∫ ds s^{3N-1} g kT
× δ[ g kT/2 + φ(q') + g kT ln s - E ]
= [ ∫ dp' δ( Σ<span class="hljs-emphasis">_i p_</span>i'^2/(2m<span class="hljs-emphasis">_i) - g kT/2 ) ] ∫ dq'
× exp{ -(3N/g)[ g kT/2 + φ(q') - E ]/(kT) }.
取g = 3N，我们得到平衡分布函数
p(p',q') = δ( Σ_</span>i p<span class="hljs-emphasis">_i'^2/(2m_</span>i) - g kT/2 ) exp[ -φ(q')/(kT) ]. (3.7)
方程(3.4)-(3.6)在坐标空间中产生正则分布。

coordinate space. Note that if we sample in real time, g must be3N- L
坐标空间中。注意，如果我们以实时采样，g必须为3N-1。

= <span class="hljs-string">C</span>' (<span class="hljs-attr">E</span> <span class="hljs-attr">-</span> <span class="hljs-attr">φ</span>)^{(<span class="hljs-attr">3N-2</span>)/<span class="hljs-attr">2</span>} = <span class="hljs-string">C</span>'' ( <span class="hljs-attr">1</span> + (⟨<span class="hljs-attr">φ</span>⟩ <span class="hljs-attr">-</span> <span class="hljs-attr">φ</span>)/(<span class="hljs-attr">3N</span> <span class="hljs-attr">k</span> <span class="hljs-attr">T</span>/<span class="hljs-attr">2</span>) )^{(<span class="hljs-attr">3N-2</span>)/<span class="hljs-attr">2</span>}, (<span class="hljs-attr">3.18</span>)
<span class="hljs-attr">其中P是3N维球体的半径</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">且E</span> = <span class="hljs-string">(3N/2)kT</span> + ⟨<span class="hljs-attr">φ</span>⟩。<span class="hljs-attr">因此</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">正则系综与HG方法13产生的系综之间的差异</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">与我们预期微正则系综与正则系综之间的差异具有相同的量级</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">对于一阶量</span>（<span class="hljs-attr">能量</span>、<span class="hljs-attr">维里等</span>），<span class="hljs-attr">通常预期N</span>^{<span class="hljs-attr">-1</span>}<span class="hljs-attr">量级的系综修正</span>；<span class="hljs-attr">对于与涨落公式相关的量</span>（<span class="hljs-attr">热容</span>、<span class="hljs-attr">压缩率等</span>），<span class="hljs-attr">预期量级为1的修正</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">21</span>,<span class="hljs-attr">22例如</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">HG方法中势能的涨落为</span>
⟨(<span class="hljs-attr">δφ</span>)^<span class="hljs-attr">2</span>⟩ = <span class="hljs-string">N</span> (<span class="hljs-attr">kT</span>)^<span class="hljs-attr">2</span> <span class="hljs-attr">3c</span>/(<span class="hljs-attr">3</span>+<span class="hljs-attr">c</span>) + <span class="hljs-attr">O</span>(<span class="hljs-attr">1</span>), (<span class="hljs-attr">3.19</span>)

**<span class="hljs-attr">virial</span>**<span class="hljs-attr">:</span> <span class="hljs-attr">维里</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">与粒子间作用力与位置乘积相关的量</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">常用于计算压力</span>。

<span class="hljs-attr">结合Andersen1的恒压MD方法</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">正则系综MD方法可以容易地扩展至TP系综</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">19这里我们使用Andersen给出的均匀膨胀公式</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">但向Parrinello和Rahman2-4的广义恒压模拟方法的扩展可以类似方式推导</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">在TP系综中</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">虚拟变量</span>(<span class="hljs-attr">q_i</span>, <span class="hljs-attr">p_i</span>, <span class="hljs-attr">s</span>, <span class="hljs-attr">V</span>, <span class="hljs-attr">t</span>)<span class="hljs-attr">通过坐标乘以V</span>^{<span class="hljs-attr">1</span>/<span class="hljs-attr">3</span>}<span class="hljs-attr">和时间乘以s进行标度</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">与实变量</span>(<span class="hljs-attr">q_i</span>', <span class="hljs-attr">p_i</span>', <span class="hljs-attr">s</span>, <span class="hljs-attr">V</span>, <span class="hljs-attr">t</span>')<span class="hljs-attr">相关联</span>（<span class="hljs-attr">V为MD胞的体积</span>）：
<span class="hljs-attr">q_i</span> = <span class="hljs-string">V^{1/3}</span> <span class="hljs-attr">q_i</span>', (<span class="hljs-attr">4.1</span>)
<span class="hljs-attr">p_i</span> = <span class="hljs-string">p_i</span>' / (<span class="hljs-attr">V</span>^{<span class="hljs-attr">1</span>/<span class="hljs-attr">3</span>} <span class="hljs-attr">s</span>), (<span class="hljs-attr">4.2</span>)
<span class="hljs-attr">t</span>' = <span class="hljs-string">∫</span> <span class="hljs-attr">dt</span> / <span class="hljs-attr">s.</span> (<span class="hljs-attr">4.3</span>)
<span class="hljs-attr">标度坐标q_i</span>'<span class="hljs-attr">的各分量值限制在0到1的范围内</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">哈密顿量为</span>
<span class="hljs-attr">H</span> = <span class="hljs-string">Σ_i</span> <span class="hljs-attr">p_i</span>^<span class="hljs-attr">2</span>/(<span class="hljs-attr">2m_i</span> <span class="hljs-attr">V</span>^{<span class="hljs-attr">2</span>/<span class="hljs-attr">3</span>} <span class="hljs-attr">s</span>^<span class="hljs-attr">2</span>) + <span class="hljs-attr">φ</span>(<span class="hljs-attr">V</span>^{<span class="hljs-attr">1</span>/<span class="hljs-attr">3</span>} <span class="hljs-attr">q</span>') + <span class="hljs-attr">p_s</span>^<span class="hljs-attr">2</span>/(<span class="hljs-attr">2Q</span>) + <span class="hljs-attr">gkT</span> <span class="hljs-attr">ln</span> <span class="hljs-attr">s</span> + <span class="hljs-attr">p_v</span>^<span class="hljs-attr">2</span>/(<span class="hljs-attr">2W</span>) + <span class="hljs-attr">P_ex</span> <span class="hljs-attr">V</span>, (<span class="hljs-attr">4.4</span>)
<span class="hljs-attr">其中p_v是V的共轭动量</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">W是体积运动的</span>“<span class="hljs-attr">质量</span>”，<span class="hljs-attr">P_ex是外部设定的压力</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">运动方程为</span>
<span class="hljs-attr">dq_i</span>/<span class="hljs-attr">dt</span> = <span class="hljs-string">∂H/∂p_i</span> = <span class="hljs-string">p_i/(m_i</span> <span class="hljs-attr">V</span>^{<span class="hljs-attr">2</span>/<span class="hljs-attr">3</span>} <span class="hljs-attr">s</span>^<span class="hljs-attr">2</span>), (<span class="hljs-attr">4.5</span>)

<span class="hljs-attr">表I.</span> <span class="hljs-attr">各种恒温方法之间的关系</span>。
<span class="hljs-attr">扩展系统方法</span>
<span class="hljs-attr">这些方程的关系列于表I</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">HLME方法14-18和Evans-Morriss方法18作为ES方法19的特例被导出</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">除此处提及的方法和Andersen1的方法外</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">其他方法似乎都不能给出严格的正则分布</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">这些方法首先在虚拟变量表述中提出</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">然后转换到实变量情形</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">虚拟变量表述是恒温方法的支柱</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">在这种形式中</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">方程保持正则</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">与正则系综等价的证明是直接的</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">然而</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">不等的时间间隔在模拟中不方便</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">实变量表述推荐用于应用</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">应记住</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">在这种情况下方程不再是正则的</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">两种恒温方法</span>（<span class="hljs-attr">ES和HLME</span>）<span class="hljs-attr">仍不理想</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">独立变量的数目列于表II</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">ES方法比等效的统计力学系综具有更多的独立变量</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">这就是ES方法对静态量给出正确结果的原因</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">但s和</span>/<span class="hljs-attr">或V的时间演化依赖于可调参数Q和</span>/<span class="hljs-attr">或W</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">这种任意性既是该方法的缺点也是优点</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">恒压MD方法7和恒温方法13</span>,<span class="hljs-attr">24</span>,<span class="hljs-attr">25中速度自相关函数的计算结果显示与普通MD方法无显著差异</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">如果对物理系统动力学的影响可忽略</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">我们可以选择参数Q和W以优化计算效率</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">在约束方法中</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">由于添加了约束</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">独立变量的数目少于统计力学表述中的数目</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">约束方法中的某些静态量并非恰好对应于正则分布</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">在正则系综中</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">只有依赖于动量的量受到影响</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">在TP系综中</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">压力约束同时依赖于体积和坐标空间</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">如果我们定义瞬时温度T_i和压力P_i为</span>

<span class="hljs-attr">Ti</span> = <span class="hljs-string">(2/(3Nk))</span> <span class="hljs-attr">Σ_i</span> <span class="hljs-attr">p_i</span>'^<span class="hljs-attr">2</span>/(<span class="hljs-attr">2m_i</span>), (<span class="hljs-attr">5.1</span>)
<span class="hljs-attr">Pi</span> = <span class="hljs-string">(1/(3V))</span> [ <span class="hljs-attr">Σ_i</span> <span class="hljs-attr">p_i</span>'^<span class="hljs-attr">2</span>/<span class="hljs-attr">m_i</span> + <span class="hljs-attr">Σ_i</span> <span class="hljs-attr">q_i</span>' · <span class="hljs-attr">F_i</span>' ], (<span class="hljs-attr">5.2</span>)
<span class="hljs-attr">T_i</span> = <span class="hljs-string">(2/(3Nk))</span> <span class="hljs-attr">Σ_i</span> <span class="hljs-attr">p_i</span>'^<span class="hljs-attr">2</span>/(<span class="hljs-attr">2m_i</span>), (<span class="hljs-attr">5.1</span>)
<span class="hljs-attr">P_i</span> = <span class="hljs-string">(1/(3V))</span> [ <span class="hljs-attr">Σ_i</span> <span class="hljs-attr">p_i</span>'^<span class="hljs-attr">2</span>/<span class="hljs-attr">m_i</span> + <span class="hljs-attr">Σ_i</span> <span class="hljs-attr">q_i</span>' · <span class="hljs-attr">F_i</span>' ], (<span class="hljs-attr">5.2</span>)

class="hljs-attr">5.3</span>) <span class="hljs-attr">and</span> (<span class="hljs-attr">5.5</span>)<span class="hljs-attr">.</span>
<span class="hljs-attr">这些量在正则系综和TP系综中的平均值和涨落为</span>
⟨<span class="hljs-attr">T_i</span>⟩ = <span class="hljs-string">T,</span> (<span class="hljs-attr">5.3</span>)
⟨(<span class="hljs-attr">T_i</span> <span class="hljs-attr">-</span> <span class="hljs-attr">T</span>)^<span class="hljs-attr">2</span>⟩ = <span class="hljs-string">(2/(3N))</span> <span class="hljs-attr">T</span>^<span class="hljs-attr">2</span>, (<span class="hljs-attr">5.4</span>)
⟨<span class="hljs-attr">P_i</span>⟩ = <span class="hljs-string">P_ex,</span> (<span class="hljs-attr">5.5</span>)
⟨(<span class="hljs-attr">P_i</span> <span class="hljs-attr">-</span> <span class="hljs-attr">P_ex</span>)^<span class="hljs-attr">2</span>⟩ = <span class="hljs-string">(2kT/(3V))</span> (∂⟨<span class="hljs-attr">P_i</span>⟩/∂<span class="hljs-attr">V</span>)^{<span class="hljs-attr">-1</span>} + <span class="hljs-attr">...</span> (<span class="hljs-attr">5.6</span>)
<span class="hljs-attr">在约束方法中</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">T_i和P_i的涨落</span>[<span class="hljs-attr">方程</span>(<span class="hljs-attr">5.4</span>)<span class="hljs-attr">和</span>(<span class="hljs-attr">5.6</span>)]<span class="hljs-attr">被抑制</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">在统计力学的表述中</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">T和P_ex是外部系统的温度和压力</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">而物理系统中的温度和压力值仅通过方程</span>(<span class="hljs-attr">5.3</span>)<span class="hljs-attr">和</span>(<span class="hljs-attr">5.5</span>)<span class="hljs-attr">在平均意义上定义</span>。

(<span class="hljs-attr">2.5</span>)]<span class="hljs-attr">中</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">并且转动的自由度数必须加到g值中</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">恒压MD方法在任何情况下都与恒焓-恒压系综有N</span>^{<span class="hljs-attr">-1</span>}<span class="hljs-attr">量级的偏差</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">这是由于体积运动的动能项p_v</span>^<span class="hljs-attr">2</span>/(<span class="hljs-attr">2W</span>)<span class="hljs-attr">所致</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">因此</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">对此项使用不同函数形式</span>[<span class="hljs-attr">例如A</span>(<span class="hljs-attr">V</span>)<span class="hljs-attr">p_v</span>^<span class="hljs-attr">2</span>]<span class="hljs-attr">的影响远不那么显著</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">4然而</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">在正则系综和TP系综方法中</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">一开始就没有N</span>^{<span class="hljs-attr">-1</span>}<span class="hljs-attr">量级的内在偏差</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">因此这些动能项的不同形式</span>（<span class="hljs-attr">例如p_s</span>^<span class="hljs-attr">2</span>/(<span class="hljs-attr">2Q</span>)<span class="hljs-attr">或p_s</span>^<span class="hljs-attr">2</span>/(<span class="hljs-attr">2Qs</span>^<span class="hljs-attr">2</span>), <span class="hljs-attr">p_v</span>^<span class="hljs-attr">2</span>/(<span class="hljs-attr">2W</span>)）<span class="hljs-attr">可能导致N</span>^{<span class="hljs-attr">-1</span>}<span class="hljs-attr">量级的偏差</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">在这些类型的计算中</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">仔细选择附加动能项的函数形式很重要</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">由于总动量和角动量守恒</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">MD方法产生的系综与统计力学系综略有偏差</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">20-22考虑上述守恒定律时的真实独立变量数在表II中用括号括出</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">实际上</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">如果采用周期性边界条件</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">MD模拟中角动量不守恒</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">这里</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">我们只讨论动量守恒定律的影响</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">动量守恒定律在用虚拟变量表示时成立</span>，

<span class="hljs-attr">这源于独立变量数目的减少</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">在TP系综的情况下</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">体积的权重因子也会改变</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">理论上</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">静态量与参数Q的值无关</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">然而</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">在实际中由于时间步数有限</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">等价方程</span>(<span class="hljs-attr">2.17</span>)<span class="hljs-attr">并不总是满足</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">Q值小时</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">与s相关的自由度倾向于与物理系统解耦</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">另一方面</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">Q值大导致相空间采样效率低下</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">最有效的计算将通过选择物理系统和变量s具有相同数量级的时间尺度来完成</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">如果我们只考虑s围绕平均值</span>⟨<span class="hljs-attr">s</span>⟩<span class="hljs-attr">的涨落</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">s</span> = <span class="hljs-string">⟨s⟩</span> + <span class="hljs-attr">δs</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">19方程</span>(<span class="hljs-attr">2.12</span>)<span class="hljs-attr">可简化为</span>

<span class="hljs-attr">这个谐振方程的频率为</span>
<span class="hljs-attr">ω</span>^<span class="hljs-attr">2</span> = <span class="hljs-string">(2gkT)/(Q</span> ⟨<span class="hljs-attr">s</span>⟩^<span class="hljs-attr">2</span>)<span class="hljs-attr">.</span> (<span class="hljs-attr">5.12</span>)
<span class="hljs-attr">我们可以选择一个Q值</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">使得方程</span>(<span class="hljs-attr">5.12</span>)<span class="hljs-attr">中的ω与物理系统速度自相关函数频谱的二阶矩具有相同的数量级</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">这个时间尺度大致对应于声波传播最近邻距离所需的时间</span>。

<span class="hljs-attr">对三种恒温MD方法进行了分析检验</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">除动量和角动量守恒的影响外</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">ES方法19在正则系综和TP系综中给出了严格的平衡分布函数</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">HLME约束方法14-18仅在坐标空间中给出正则分布</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">HLME方法和Evans-Morriss方法均通过施加约束从ES方法的表述导出</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">另一方面</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">Haile-Gupta方法13不给出严格的正则分布</span>。
<span class="hljs-attr">虚拟变量表述最适合于证明与统计力学系综的等价性</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">基于实变量表述的方程</span>[<span class="hljs-attr">方程</span>(<span class="hljs-attr">2.19</span>)<span class="hljs-attr">-</span>(<span class="hljs-attr">2.25</span>)<span class="hljs-attr">或HLME方法</span>]<span class="hljs-attr">推荐用于应用</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">将MD方法扩展到微正则系综以外的系综以统一的方式表述</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">通过引入实变量和虚拟变量</span>，<span class="hljs-attr">恒压MD方法由坐标的标度生成</span>。<span class="hljs-attr">恒温MD方法由时间的标度得到</span>。
